313	(RUN ? it : it.skip)(`WWW tail replay ${FROM}..${TO}`, async () => {
314	  const t0 = Date.now();
315	  const producersBox: { shrineSpots: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null; oceanTreasures: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null } = { shrineSpots: null, oceanTreasures: null };
316	  const plans = makePlans(producersBox);
317	  const world = new World(W, H, SEED);
318	  const rows: Row[] = [];
319	
320	  const slots = [...plans.keys()].filter(s => {
321	    const ds = plans.get(s)!.steps.map(x => x.dump ?? 999).filter(d => d < 999);
322	    return ds.some(d => d >= FROM && d <= TO);
323	  }).sort((a, b) => a - b);
324	  for (const s of slots) {
325	    const plan = plans.get(s)!;
326	    if (plan.steps.some(x => x.dump === 61 || x.dump === 62) && !producersBox.shrineSpots) {
327	      const producers = computeProducers();
328	      producersBox.shrineSpots = producers.shrineSpots; producersBox.oceanTreasures = producers.oceanTreasures;
329	      console.log(`producers: shrineSpots=${producersBox.shrineSpots.length} oceanTreasures=${producersBox.oceanTreasures.length}`);
330	    }
331	    const entry = boundary(plan.entry);
332	    const gs: GenState = structuredClone(entry.gs);
333	    restoreGenSolid(entry.gensolid);
334	    __setGenStateForProbe(gs);
335	    const chests = structuredClone(entry.chests) as Chests;
336	    let prev: PrevOut | null = null;
337	    for (const step of plan.steps) {
338	      const built = prev ? buildHybrid(step.base, prev) : buildHybrid(step.base, entry);
339	      if (built.revMiss) console.log(`  [revMiss] base=${step.base} cells=${built.revMiss}`);
340	      const rng = new RNG(SEED);
341	      await step.run(built.st, gs, rng, chests, world);
342	      if (step.dump !== undefined && step.dump >= FROM && step.dump <= TO) {
343	        rows.push(diffStore(`jsSlot${s}`, step.dump, built.st));
344	      }
345	      prev = prevOf(built.st);
346	    }
347	    __setGenStateForProbe(null);
348	  }
349	  for (const r of rows) printRow(r);
350	  const bad = rows.filter(r => !isClean(r));
351	  console.log(`\n★ 尾段裁决 ${FROM}..${TO}: ${rows.length} 槽对拍，自因差槽 ${bad.length} 个`);
352	  for (const r of bad) console.log(`  ✗ #${r.idx} ${DUMPNAME.get(r.idx)} first=${r.first} pairs=${r.pairs.slice(0, 4).map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}`);
353	  console.log(`done ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s`);
354	}, 560_000);
355

desc.set(i, d.trim()); }\n  }\n  console.log(`diff cells = ${diffCells.length}`);\n\n  // 连通域聚类(8 邻域)\n  const seen = new Uint8Array(N);\n  const clusters: Array<{ cells: number[]; x0: number; x1: number; y0: number; y1: number }> = [];\n  for (const c0 of diffCells) {\n    if (seen[c0]) continue;\n    const stack = [c0];\n    seen[c0] = 1;\n    const cells: number[] = [];\n    let x0 = W, x1 = 0, y0 = H, y1 = 0;\n    while (stack.length) {\n      const i = stack.pop()!;\n      cells.push(i);\n      const x = i % W, y = (i / W) | 0;\n      if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x;\n      if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y;\n      for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) {\n        for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {\n          if (!dx && !dy) continue;\n          const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;\n          if (nx < 0 || nx >= W || ny < 0 || ny >= H) continue;\n          const ni = ny * W + nx;\n          if (!seen[ni] && desc.has(ni)) { seen[ni] = 1; stack.push(ni); }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    clusters.push({ cells, x0, x1, y0, y1 });\n  }\n  clusters.sort((a, b) => b.cells.length - a.cells.length);\n  console.log(`clusters = ${clusters.length}`);\n  for (let ci = 0; ci < Math.min(clusters.length, 40); ci++) {\n    const cl = clusters[ci]!;\n    const pairCount = new Map<string, number>();\n    for (const i of cl.cells) {\n      for (const tok of desc.get(i)!.split(' ')) pairCount.set(tok, (pairCount.get(tok) ?? 0) + 1);\n    }\n    const top = [...pairCount.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 8).map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ');\n    const ex = cl.cells[0]!;\n    console.log(`#${ci} n=${cl.cells.length} bbox=[${cl.x0},${cl.y0}]-[${cl.x1},${cl.y1}] w=${cl.x1 - cl.x0 + 1} h=${cl.y1 - cl.y0 + 1} :: ${top} :: eg(${ex % W},${(ex / W) | 0}) ${desc.get(ex)}`);\n  }\n}, 300_000);\n"
}
```

x0: number; x1: number; y0: number; y1: number }> {\n  const seen = new Uint8Array(N);\n  const out: Array<{ n: number; x0: number; x1: number; y0: number; y1: number }> = [];\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    if (!mask[i] || seen[i]) continue;\n    const stack = [i];\n    seen[i] = 1;\n    let n = 0, x0 = W, x1 = 0, y0 = H, y1 = 0;\n    while (stack.length) {\n      const p = stack.pop()!;\n      n++;\n      const x = p % W, y = (p / W) | 0;\n      if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x;\n      if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y;\n      for (let dy = -pad; dy <= pad; dy++) {\n        for (let dx = -pad; dx <= pad; dx++) {\n          const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;\n          if (nx < 0 || nx >= W || ny < 0 || ny >= H) continue;\n          const ni = ny * W + nx;\n          if (!seen[ni] && mask[ni]) { seen[ni] = 1; stack.push(ni); }\n        }\n      }\n    }\n    out.push({ n, x0, x1, y0, y1 });\n  }\n  out.sort((a, b) => b.n - a.n);\n  return out;\n}\n\n(itIs ? it : it.skip)('cccc segtrace', async () => {\n  // ---- golden 侧:100→101 增量里抽各段结构 ----\n  const gt0 = b16(100, 'typ'), ga0 = b8(100, 'act'), gw0 = b16(100, 'wal');\n  const gt1 = b16(101, 'typ'), ga1 = b8(101, 'act'), gw1 = b16(101, 'wal');\n  // 薄冰斑:golden101 新增 162 连通域(halo 12 —— 斑间距>12 为不同斑)\n  const ice = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) if (gt1[i] === 162 && gt0[i] !== 162) ice[i] = 1;\n  console.log('== golden thin-ice patches (162 new, halo 12):');\n  for (const c of cluster(ice, 12).slice(0, 10)) console.log(`   n=${c.n} bbox=[${c.x0},${c.y0}]-[${c.x1},${c.y1}] ctr≈(${(c.x0 + c.x1) >> 1},${(c.y0 + c.y1) >> 1})`);\n  // 死箱:golden101 新增 467\n  const dmc = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) if (gt1[i] === 467 && gt0[i] !== 467) dmc[i] = 1;\n  console.log('== golden dead-mans chests (467 new, halo 4):');\n  for (const c of cluster(dmc, 4).slice(0, 15)) console.log(`   n=${c.n} bbox=[${c.x0},${c.y0}]-[${c.x1},${c.y1}] ctr≈(${c.x0},${c.y0})`);\n  // 桃花心木:golden101 新增 383(halo 20)\n  const mah = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) if (gt1[i] === 383) mah[i] = 1;\n  console.log('== golden mahogany (383 total, halo 20):');\n  for (const c of cluster(mah, 20).slice(0, 10)) console.log(`   n=${c.n} bbox=[${c.x0},${c.y0}]-[${c.x1},${c.y1}]`);\n  // 营地墙:golden101 新增墙 196-199/40/171/178/180/204-207(halo 8)\n  const camp = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    const wv = gw1[i]!;\n    if (wv !== gw0[i] && (wv === 40 || wv === 171 || wv === 178 || wv === 180 || (wv >= 196 && wv <= 199) || (wv >= 204 && wv <= 207))) camp[i] = 1;\n  }\n  console.log('== golden campsite walls (new, halo 8):');\n  for (const c of cluster(camp, 8).slice(0, 15)) console.log(`   n=${c.n} bbox=[${c.x0},${c.y0}]-[${c.x1},${c.y1}] ctr≈(${(c.x0 + c.x1) >> 1},${(c.y0 + c.y1) >> 1})`);\n  // 轨道:golden101 新增 314(halo 30 连成线路)\n  const trk = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) if (gt1[i] === 314 && gt0[i] !== 314) trk[i] = 1;\n  console.log('== golden tracks (314 new, halo 30):');\n  for (const c of cluster(trk, 30).slice(0, 8)) console.log(`   n=${c.n} bbox=[${c.x0},${c.y0}]-[${c.x1},${c.y1}]`);\n\n  // ---- JS 侧:重放 + segProbe 流 ----\n  const REV = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n    const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n    if (typeof s === 'number' && !REV.has(s)) REV.set(s, i);\n  }\n  const gsB = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/gs.json`, 'utf8')) as GenState;\n  const gensolid = new Map(JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/gensolid.json`, 'utf8')) as Array<[number, boolean]>);\n  const fw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/frames.bin`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const fx = new Uint16Array(N), fy = new Uint16Array(N), wire = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let q = 0; q < fw.length; q += 4) { const i = fw[q]!; fx[i] = fw[q + 1]!; fy[i] = fw[q + 2]!; wire[i] = fw[q + 3]! & 0x1f; }\n  const iw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/ids35.bin`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const ids35 = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let q = 0; q < iw.length; q += 2) ids35.set(iw[q]!, iw[q + 1]!);\n  const chests = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/chests.json`, 'utf8')) as unknown[];\n  const st = new TileStore(W, H);\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    const act = ga0[i]!;\n    st.flags[i] = act;\n    if (act || gt0[i]) {\n      const sh = gt0[i]!;\n      let id = ids35.has(i) ? ids35.get(i)! : -1;\n      if (id < 0 || SHEET[id] !== sh) id = REV.get(sh) ?? -1;\n      st.type[i] = id < 0 ? 0 : id;\n    }\n    st.wall[i] = gw0[i]!;\n    st.liquid[i] = b8(100, 'liq')[i]!;\n    st.liquidType[i] = st.liquid[i] ? b8(100, 'lty')[i]! + 1 : 0;\n    st.half[i] = b8(100, 'half')[i]!; st.slope[i] = b8(100, 'slope')[i]!;\n    st.wire[i] = (b8(100, 'inact')[i]! ? 0x20 : 0) | wire[i];\n    st.frameX[i] = fx[i]; st.frameY[i] = fy[i];\n  }\n  restoreGenSolid(gensolid);\n  const gs = structuredClone(gsB) as GenState;\n  __setGenStateForProbe(gs);\n  const rng = new RNG(SEED);\n  let draws = 0;\n  const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(rng) as Record<string, unknown>;\n  for (const name of ['next', 'int', 'range', 'nextInt']) {\n    const orig = proto[name] as (...a: unknown[]) => unknown;\n    if (typeof orig !== 'function') continue;\n    (rng as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[name] = function (this: unknown, ...args: unknown[]) {\n      draws++;\n      return orig.apply(this === rng ? rng : this, args);\n    };\n  }\n  const events: Array<{ d: number; name: string; detail: unknown }> = [];\n  const G = globalThis as { __swSeg?: (name: string, detail?: unknown) => void };\n  G.__swSeg = (name, detail) => {\n    events.push({ d: draws, name, detail });\n  };\n  runMicroBiomesPass(st, rng, gs, chests as never);\n  delete G.__swSeg;\n  __setGenStateForProbe(null);\n  // 打印流(截流:iceTry/campTry/trapTry/treeTry 多于 60 条只打前 60)\n  console.log(`== JS seg trace (draws total=${draws}, events=${events.length}):`);\n  let nTry = 0;\n  for (const e of events) {\n    if (e.name === 'iceTry' || e.name === 'campTry' || e.name === 'trapTry' || e.name === 'treeTry' || e.name === 'swordTry') {\n      nTry++;\n      if (nTry > 80) { if (nTry === 81) console.log('   …(try 流截断)'); continue; }\n    }\n    console.log(`   [${e.d}] ${e.name} ${JSON.stringify(e.detail)}`);\n  }\n}, 300_000);\n"
}

	private static bool IsAGoodSpot(Point position)

		}
		if (tile.frameX / 36 != 1)
		{
			return false;
		}
		tile = Main.tile[position.X, position.Y + 2];
		if (!WorldGen.CanBeClearedDuringGeneration(tile.type, position.X, position.Y + 2))
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (WorldGen.countWires(position.X, position.Y, 20) > 0)
		{
			return false;
		}
		if (WorldGen.countTiles(position.X, position.Y, jungle: false, lavaOk: true) < 40)
		{
			return false;
		}
		return true;
	}

	public static void nextCount(int x, int y, bool jungle = false, bool lavaOk = false)
	{
		if (numTileCount >= maxTileCount)
		{
			return;
		}
		if (x <= 1 || x >= Main.maxTilesX - 1 || y <= 1 || y >= Main.maxTilesY - 1)
		{

		else
		{

				numTileCount = maxTileCount;
				return;
			}
			if (Main.tile[x, y].shimmer() && Main.tile[x, y].liquid > 0)
			{
				numTileCount = maxTileCount;
				return;
			}
			if (!jungle)
			{
				if (Main.tile[x, y].wall != 0)
				{
					numTileCount = maxTileCount;
					return;
				}
				if (!lavaOk)
				{
					if (Main.tile[x, y].lava() && Main.tile[x, y].liquid > 0)
					{
						lavaCount++;
						numTileCount = maxTileCount;
						return;
					}
				}
				else if (Main.tile[x, y].lava() && Main.tile[x, y].liquid > 0)
				{
					lavaCount++;
				}
			}
			if (Main.tile[x, y].active())
			{
				if (Main.tile[x, y].type == 70)
				{
					shroomCount++;
				}
				if (Main.tile[x, y].type == 1)
				{
					rockCount++;
				}
				if (Main.tile[x, y].type == 147 || Main.tile[x, y].type == 161)
				{
					iceCount++;
				}
				if (Main.tile[x, y].type == 53 || Main.tile[x, y].type == 396 || Main.tile[x, y].type == 397)
				{
					sandCount++;
				}
			}
			if (!SolidTile(x, y))
			{
				CountedTiles.Add(new Point(x, y), value: true);
				numTileCount++;
				nextCount(x - 1, y, jungle, lavaOk);
				nextCount(x + 1, y, jungle, lavaOk);
				nextCount(x, y - 1, jungle, lavaOk);
				nextCount(x, y + 1, jungle, lavaOk);
			}
		}
	}

 "content": "// CCCC 批·诊断⑤:(1129,537) 逐门测量 + countTiles 修正版对照。一次性探针。\nimport { it } from 'vitest';\nimport { readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';\nimport { RNG } from '../src/core/rng';\nimport { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';\nimport type { GenState } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/GenState';\nimport { restoreGenSolid, vanGenSolidType } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/GenSolid';\nimport { __setGenStateForProbe } from '../src/world/gen/WorldGen';\nimport { DeadMansChestBiome } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/MicroBiomesPass';\nimport { NOT_CLEARABLE } from '../src/world/gen/vanilla/TileRunner';\n\nconst G2 = '/tmp/sw-slp/g';\nconst BDIR = '/tmp/www-b';\nconst W = 4200, H = 1200, N = W * H;\nconst SEED = 9293480;\n\nconst SHEET = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);\nfor (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n  if (typeof s === 'number') SHEET[i] = s;\n}\nconst DUMPNAME = new Map<number, string>();\nfor (const f of readdirSync(G2)) { const m = /^(\\d{3})_(.+)\\.typ$/.exec(f); if (m) DUMPNAME.set(Number(m[1]), m[2]); }\nconst gPath = (idx: number, ext: string) => `${G2}/${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${DUMPNAME.get(idx)}.${ext}`;\nconst b8 = (idx: number, e: string) => new Uint8Array(readFileSync(gPath(idx, e)).buffer.slice(0));\nconst b16 = (idx: number, e: string) => new Uint16Array(readFileSync(gPath(idx, e)).buffer.slice(0));\n\nconst itIs = process.env.SW_CCCC === '5';\n\n(itIs ? it : it.skip)('cccc gate measure', async () => {\n  const gt = b16(100, 'typ'), ga = b8(100, 'act'), gw = b16(100, 'wal'), gl = b8(100, 'liq');\n  const ghf = b8(100, 'half'), gsl = b8(100, 'slope'), glt = b8(100, 'lty'), gia = b8(100, 'inact');\n  const REV = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n    const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n    if (typeof s === 'number' && !REV.has(s)) REV.set(s, i);\n  }\n  const gsB = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/gs.json`, 'utf8')) as GenState;\n  const gensolid = new Map(JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/gensolid.json`, 'utf8')) as Array<[number, boolean]>);\n  const fw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/frames.bin`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const fx = new Uint16Array(N), fy = new Uint16Array(N), wire = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let q = 0; q < fw.length; q += 4) { const i = fw[q]!; fx[i] = fw[q + 1]!; fy[i] = fw[q + 2]!; wire[i] = fw[q + 3]! & 0x1f; }\n  const iw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/ids35.bin`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const ids35 = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let q = 0; q < iw.length; q += 2) ids35.set(iw[q]!, iw[q + 1]!);\n  const chestList = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/chests.json`, 'utf8')) as Array<{ x: number; y: number }>;\n\n  const st = new TileStore(W, H);\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    const act = ga[i]!;\n    st.flags[i] = act;\n    if (act || gt[i]) {\n      const sh = gt[i]!;\n      let id = ids35.has(i) ? ids35.get(i)! : -1;\n      if (id < 0 || SHEET[id] !== sh) id = REV.get(sh) ?? -1;\n      st.type[i] = id < 0 ? 0 : id;\n    }\n    st.wall[i] = gw[i]!; st.liquid[i] = gl[i]!;\n    st.liquidType[i] = gl[i] ? glt[i]! + 1 : 0;\n    st.half[i] = ghf[i]!; st.slope[i] = gsl[i]!;\n    st.wire[i] = (gia[i]! ? 0x20 : 0) | wire[i];\n    st.frameX[i] = fx[i]; st.frameY[i] = fy[i];\n  }\n  restoreGenSolid(gensolid);\n  const gs = structuredClone(gsB) as GenState;\n  __setGenStateForProbe(gs);\n\n  // 修正版 countTiles(SolidTile 1:1):active && genSolid && !solidTop && !half && slope==0 && !inAct\n  const solidTopTable = (t: number): boolean => !!TILE_DEFS[t]?.platform;\n  const solidTileVan = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {\n    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return true;\n    const i = st.idx(x, y);\n    return st.flags[i] !== 0 && vanGenSolidType(st.type[i]!) && !solidTopTable(st.type[i]!)\n      && st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0 && !(st.wire[i]! & 0x20);\n  };\n  const MAX = 3500;\n  function countTilesVan(x0: number, y0: number): number {\n    let count = 0;\n    const seen = new Set<number>();\n    const stack: Array<[number, number]> = [[x0, y0]];\n    while (stack.length > 0) {\n      if (count >= MAX) return count;\n      const [x, y] = stack.pop()!;\n      if (x <= 1 || x >= W - 1 || y <= 1 || y >= H - 1) return MAX;\n      const k = y * W + x;\n      if (seen.has(k)) continue;\n      seen.add(k);\n      const i = st.idx(x, y);\n      if (st.wall[i] === 244) return MAX;\n      if (st.liquid[i] > 0 && st.liquidType[i] === 4) return MAX;\n      if (st.wall[i] !== 0) return MAX;\n      if (!solidTileVan(x, y)) { count++; stack.push([x - 1, y], [x + 1, y], [x, y - 1], [x, y + 1]); }\n    }\n    return count;\n  }\n\n  const dmc = new DeadMansChestBiome(st, new RNG(SEED), gs, chestList);\n  const D = dmc as unknown as { countWiresP(x: number, y: number, s: number): number; countTilesP(x: number, y: number): number };\n  for (const [x, y] of [[1129, 537], [1546, 552]] as Array<[number, number]>) {\n    const i = st.idx(x, y);\n    const below = st.idx(x, y + 2);\n    console.log(`\\n== (${x},${y})`);\n    console.log(`   type=${TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]!]?.key} fx=${st.frameX[i]} fy=${st.frameY[i]} | below(y+2) type=${TILE_DEFS[st.type[below]!]?.key} notClearable=${NOT_CLEARABLE.has(st.type[below]!)}`);\n    console.log(`   countWires(±20)=${D.countWiresP(x, y, 20)} | countTiles(JS现行)=${D.countTilesP(x, y)} | countTiles(修正SolidTile)=${countTilesVan(x, y)}`);\n  }\n  // 修正版全候选重扫:哪些箱子过/不过\n  const pass: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n  for (const c of chestList) {\n    if (!st.inBounds(c.x, c.y)) continue;\n    if (countTilesVan(c.x, c.y) < 40) continue;\n    pass.push([c.x, c.y]);\n  }\n  console.log(`\\ncountTiles修正版 ≥40 的箱子数=${pass.length}`);\n  const i1129 = pass.findIndex(c => c[0] === 1129 && c[1] === 537);\n  const i1546 = pass.findIndex(c => c[0] === 1546 && c[1] === 552);\n  console.log(`(1129,537) 在修正版 flood 候选中的位置=${i1129};(1546,552)=${i1546}`);\n  // (1129,537) 周边实心差异格(JS 现行判实心 vs 修正判非实心)——找 inActive/half 类\n  let inactNear = 0, halfNear = 0;\n  for (let dx = -20; dx <= 20; dx++) for (let dy = -20; dy <= 20; dy++) {\n    const px = 1129 + dx, py = 537 + dy;\n    if (!st.inBounds(px, py)) continue;\n    const i = st.idx(px, py);\n    if (!st.flags[i]) continue;\n    if (st.wire[i]! & 0x20) inactNear++;\n    if (st.half[i]) halfNear++;\n  }\n  console.log(`(1129,537) ±20 内 inActive 格=${inactNear}, halfBrick 格=${halfNear}`);\n  __setGenStateForProbe(null);\n}, 300_000);\n"
}
```

27	  wire: Uint8Array;
28	}

30	
31	/** GenState 深克隆（typed array → 普通 array，JSON 可序列化） */
32	function serGs(gs: unknown): unknown {
33	  if (gs === null || typeof gs !== 'object') return gs;
34	  if (gs instanceof Int32Array || gs instanceof Uint8Array || gs instanceof Float32Array) return Array.from(gs);
35	  if (Array.isArray(gs)) return gs.map(serGs);
36	  const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
37	  for (const k of Object.keys(gs as Record<string, unknown>)) out[k] = serGs((gs as Record<string, unknown>)[k]);
38	  return out;
39	}
40	
41	let capCount = 0;
42	function capture(key: string, store: StoreLike, surface: Float32Array, chests: unknown): void {
43	  const dir = `${OUT}/${key}`;
44	  mkdirSync(dir, { recursive: true });
45	  const gs = lastGenState();
46	  writeFileSync(`${dir}/gs.json`, JSON.stringify(serGs(gs)));
47	  writeFileSync(`${dir}/gensolid.json`, JSON.stringify([...snapshotGenSolid().entries()]));
48	  writeFileSync(`${dir}/surface.f32`, Buffer.from(surface.buffer, surface.byteOffset, surface.byteLength));
49	  // 稀疏 frames+wire：仅非零格
50	  const cells: number[] = [];
51	  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
52	    if (store.frameX[i] || store.frameY[i] || store.wire[i]) cells.push(i, store.frameX[i], store.frameY[i], store.wire[i]);
53	  }
54	  const fw = new Uint32Array(cells.length);
55	  for (let q = 0; q < cells.length; q++) fw[q] = cells[q];
56	  writeFileSync(`${dir}/frames.bin`, Buffer.from(fw.buffer));
57	  // sheet 3/5 格的内部 id（mushroom/flower/tallgrass 共享 vanilla type 3；tree 双 id）
58	  const ids: number[] = [];
59	  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
60	    if (!store.flags[i]) continue;
61	    const sh = SHEET[store.type[i]];
62	    if (sh === 3 || sh === 5) ids.push(i, store.type[i]);
63	  }
64	  const iw = new Uint32Array(ids.length);
65	  for (let q = 0; q < ids.length; q++) iw[q] = ids[q];
66	  writeFileSync(`${dir}/ids35.bin`, Buffer.from(iw.buffer));
67	  writeFileSync(`${dir}/chests.json`, JSON.stringify(serGs(chests) ?? []));
68	  capCount++;
69	  console.log(`[cap] ${key}: framesCells=${cells.length / 4} ids35=${ids.length / 2}`);
70	}
71	
72	const srcCache = new Map<string, string[]>();
73	function labelOf(file: string, line: number): string {
74	  let src = srcCache.get(file);
75	  if (!src) { try { src = readSrc(file); } catch { src = []; } srcCache.set(file, src); }
76	  for (let l = Math.min(line, src.length) - 1; l >= 0 && l >= line - 40; l--) {
77	    const m = src[l]!.match(/▶([^（(]+?)[（(\s]/);
78	    if (m) return m[1].trim();
79	  }
80	  return '';
81	}
82	const srcMap = new Map<string, string[]>();
83	function readSrc(file: string): string[] {
84	  let s = srcMap.get(file);
85	  if (!s) { s = _readFileSyncUtf8(file).split('\n'); srcMap.set(file, s); }
86	  return s;
87	}
88	import { readFileSync as _readFileSyncUtf8 } from 'node:fs';
89	function _readFileSyncUtf8(f: string): string { return _readFileSyncUtf8(f, 'utf8'); }
90	
91	(RUN ? it : it.skip)('WWW phase1 capture', async () => {
92	  mkdirSync(OUT, { recursive: true });
93	  // ① 槽末捕获
94	  let surfaceRef: Float32Array | null = null;
95	  let chestsRef: unknown = null;
96	  // ② 中途捕获：reseed 栈标签 ▶JungleShrines / ▶OceanCaves
97	  const RNGp = RNG.prototype as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
98	  const oReseed = RNGp.reseed as (this: unknown, s: number) => void;
99	  const midDone = new Set<string>();
100	  let storeRef: StoreLike | null = null;
101	  RNGp.reseed = function (this: unknown, s: number) {
102	    if (storeRef && surfaceRef) {
103	      const stack = new Error().stack ?? '';
104	      for (const line of stack.split('\n')) {
105	        const m = line.match(/((?:\/|[\w./-]*)src\/[^\s()]+\.ts):(\d+):\d+/);
106	        if (m && !m[1].includes('/core/rng') && !m[1].includes('_wwwcap')) {
107	          const lab = labelOf(m[1], Number(m[2]));
108	          if ((lab === 'JungleShrines' || lab === 'OceanCaves') && !midDone.has(lab)) {
109	            midDone.add(lab);
110	            capture(`m_${lab}`, storeRef, surfaceRef, chestsRef);
111	          }
112	          break;
113	        }
114	      }
115	    }
116	    return oReseed.call(this, s);
117	  };
118	  const t0 = Date.now();
119	  try {
120	    await generateWorld({
121	      width: W, height: H, seedText: String(SEED), evil: 0,
122	      onWorldPartial: (w, i, name) => {
123	        if (!storeRef) {
124	          storeRef = w.store as never as StoreLike;
125	          surfaceRef = (w as unknown as { surface?: Float32Array }).surface ?? new Float32Array(W);
126	        }
127	        // surface 是 GenCtx 的（不在 world 上）——槽末重算逐列首实心行即可（仅探针参考用）
128	        chestsRef = w.chests;
129	        capture(`s${String(i).padStart(2, '0')}`, w.store as never as StoreLike, surfaceRef, w.chests);
130	        void name;
131	      },
132	    });
133	  } finally { RNGp.reseed = oReseed; }
134	  console.log(`captured ${capCount} boundaries in ${((Date.now() - t0) / 1000).toFixed(0)}s`);
135	}, 560_000);
136

	[JsonProperty("NumberOfBoulderTraps")]
	private IntRange _numberOfBoulderTraps = new IntRange(2, 4);

	[JsonProperty("NumberOfStepsBetweenBoulderTraps")]
	private IntRange _numberOfStepsBetweenBoulderTraps = new IntRange(2, 4);

	public override bool Place(Point origin, StructureMap structures, GenerationProgress progress)
	{
		if (!IsAGoodSpot(origin))
		{
			return false;
		}
		ClearCaches();
		Point position = new Point(origin.X, origin.Y + 1);
		FindBoulderTrapSpots(position);
		FindDartTrapSpots(position);
		FindExplosiveTrapSpots(position);
		if (!AreThereEnoughTraps())
		{
			return false;
		}
		TurnGoldChestIntoDeadMansChest(origin);
		foreach (DartTrapPlacementAttempt dartTrapPlacementSpot in _dartTrapPlacementSpots)
		{
			ActuallyPlaceDartTrap(dartTrapPlacementSpot.position, dartTrapPlacementSpot.directionX, dartTrapPlacementSpot.x, dartTrapPlacementSpot.y, dartTrapPlacementSpot.xPush, dartTrapPlacementSpot.t);
		}
		foreach (WirePlacementAttempt wirePlacementSpot in _wirePlacementSpots)
		{
			PlaceWireLine(wirePlacementSpot.position, wirePlacementSpot.dirX, wirePlacementSpot.dirY, wirePlacementSpot.steps);
		}
		foreach (BoulderPlacementAttempt boulderPlacementSpot in _boulderPlacementSpots)
		{
			ActuallyPlaceBoulderTrap(boulderPlacementSpot.position, boulderPlacementSpot.yPush, boulderPlacementSpot.requiredHeight, boulderPlacementSpot.bestType);
		}
		foreach (ExplosivePlacementAttempt item in _explosivePlacementAttempt)
		{
			ActuallyPlaceExplosive(item.position);
		}
		PlaceWiresForExplosives(origin);
		return true;
	}

	private void PlaceWiresForExplosives(Point origin)
	{
		if (_explosivePlacementAttempt.Count <= 0)
		{
			return;
		}
		PlaceWireLine(origin, 0, 1, _explosivePlacementAttempt[0].position.Y - origin.Y);
		int num = _explosivePlacementAttempt[0].position.X;
		int num2 = _explosivePlacementAttempt[0].position.X;
		int y = _explosivePlacementAttempt[0].position.Y;
		for (int i = 1; i < _explosivePlacementAttempt.Count; i++)
		{
			int x = _explosivePlacementAttempt[i].position.X;
			if (num > x)
			{
				num = x;
			}
			if (num2 < x)
			{
				num2 = x;
			}
		}
		PlaceWireLine(new Point(num, y), 1, 0, num2 - num);
	}

	private bool AreThereEnoughTraps()
	{
		if (_boulderPlacementSpots.Count >= 1 || _explosivePlacementAttempt.Count >= 1)
		{
			return _dartTrapPlacementSpots.Count >= 1;
		}
		return false;
	}

	private void ClearCaches()
	{
		_dartTrapPlacementSpots.Clear();
		_wirePlacementSpots.Clear();
		_boulderPlacementSpots.Clear();
		_explosivePlacementAttempt.Clear();
	}

it : it.skip)('cccc strip replay', async () => {\n  // golden 陷阱证据:两箱 ±25 窗在 57→58 / 75→76 的增量\n  for (const [cx, cy] of [[4055, 500], [1546, 552]] as Array<[number, number]>) {\n    for (const [a, b] of [[57, 58], [75, 76]] as Array<[number, number]>) {\n      const ta = b16(a, 'typ'), aa = b8(a, 'act'), tb = b16(b, 'typ'), ab = b8(b, 'act');\n      const chg: string[] = [];\n      for (let y = cy - 25; y <= cy + 25; y++) for (let x = cx - 25; x <= cx + 25; x++) {\n        const i = y * W + x;\n        const va = aa[i]! ? ta[i]! : 0, vb = ab[i]! ? tb[i]! : 0;\n        if (va !== vb) chg.push(`(${x},${y})${va}→${vb}`);\n      }\n      console.log(`golden ${a}→${b} near (${cx},${cy}): ${chg.length} 处 ${chg.slice(0, 12).join(' ')}`);\n    }\n  }\n\n  // 基座重建\n  const gt = b16(100, 'typ'), ga = b8(100, 'act'), gw = b16(100, 'wal'), gl = b8(100, 'liq');\n  const ghf = b8(100, 'half'), gsl = b8(100, 'slope'), glt = b8(100, 'lty'), gia = b8(100, 'inact');\n  const REV = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n    const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n    if (typeof s === 'number' && !REV.has(s)) REV.set(s, i);\n  }\n  const gsB = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/gs.json`, 'utf8')) as GenState;\n  const gensolid = new Map(JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/gensolid.json`, 'utf8')) as Array<[number, boolean]>);\n  const fw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/frames.bin`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const fx = new Uint16Array(N), fy = new Uint16Array(N), wire = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let q = 0; q < fw.length; q += 4) { const i = fw[q]!; fx[i] = fw[q + 1]!; fy[i] = fw[q + 2]!; wire[i] = fw[q + 3]! & 0x1f; }\n  // ★剔除幻影 wire 带 x∈[1122,1124],y∈[517,541](s30→s31 写入的 JS#76 债)\n  let stripped = 0;\n  for (let y = 517; y <= 541; y++) for (const x of [1122, 1123, 1124]) {\n    const i = y * W + x;\n    if (wire[i]! & 15) { wire[i] = 0; stripped++; }\n  }\n  console.log(`\\nstripped ${stripped} phantom wire cells`);\n  const iw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/ids35.bin`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const ids35 = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let q = 0; q < iw.length; q += 2) ids35.set(iw[q]!, iw[q + 1]!);\n  const chests = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/chests.json`, 'utf8')) as unknown[];\n  const st = new TileStore(W, H);\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    const act = ga[i]!;\n    st.flags[i] = act;\n    if (act || gt[i]) {\n      const sh = gt[i]!;\n      let id = ids35.has(i) ? ids35.get(i)! : -1;\n      if (id < 0 || SHEET[id] !== sh) id = REV.get(sh) ?? -1;\n      st.type[i] = id < 0 ? 0 : id;\n    }\n    st.wall[i] = gw[i]!; st.liquid[i] = gl[i]!;\n    st.liquidType[i] = gl[i] ? glt[i]! + 1 : 0;\n    st.half[i] = ghf[i]!; st.slope[i] = gsl[i]!;\n    st.wire[i] = (gia[i]! ? 0x20 : 0) | wire[i];\n    st.frameX[i] = fx[i]; st.frameY[i] = fy[i];\n  }\n  restoreGenSolid(gensolid);\n  const gs = structuredClone(gsB) as GenState;\n  __setGenStateForProbe(gs);\n  const rng = new RNG(SEED);\n  let draws = 0;\n  const proto = Object.getPrototypeOf(rng) as Record<string, unknown>;\n  for (const name of ['next', 'int', 'range', 'nextInt']) {\n    const orig = proto[name] as (...a: unknown[]) => unknown;\n    if (typeof orig !== 'function') continue;\n    (rng as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[name] = function (this: unknown, ...args: unknown[]) {\n      draws++;\n      return orig.apply(this === rng ? rng : this, args);\n    };\n  }\n  const events: Array<{ d: number; name: string; detail: unknown }> = [];\n  const G = globalThis as { __swSeg?: (name: string, detail?: unknown) => void };\n  G.__swSeg = (name, detail) => events.push({ d: draws, name, detail });\n  runMicroBiomesPass(st, rng, gs, chests as never);\n  delete G.__swSeg;\n  __setGenStateForProbe(null);\n  let iceTries = 0;\n  for (const e of events) {\n    if (e.name === 'deadCands') console.log(`[${e.d}] deadCands(${(e.detail as number[][]).length}): ${JSON.stringify(e.detail)}`);\n    if (e.name === 'deadCount') console.log(`[${e.d}] deadCount=${e.detail}`);\n    if (e.name === 'iceCount') console.log(`[${e.d}] iceCount=${e.detail}`);\n    if (e.name === 'iceTry') { iceTries++; if (iceTries <= 12) console.log(`[${e.d}] iceTry ${JSON.stringify(e.detail)}`); }\n    if (e.name === 'swordAttempts') console.log(`[${e.d}] swordAttempts=${e.detail}`);\n    if (e.name === 'campCount') console.log(`[${e.d}] campCount=${e.detail}`);\n  }\n  console.log(`iceTry total=${iceTries}, draws total=${draws}`);\n  // 剔除后 467 落点(与 golden 11 箱对拍)\n  const t101 = b16(101, 'typ');\n  const js467 = new Set<string>();\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    if ((st.flags[i]! & 1) && SHEET[st.type[i]!] === 467) js467.add(`${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}`);\n  }\n  const anchors: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n  for (const k of js467) {\n    const [x, y] = k.split(',').map(Number);\n    if (!js467.has(`${x - 1},${y}`) && !js467.has(`${x},${y - 1}`)) anchors.push([x, y]);\n  }\n  console.log(`JS trapified anchors after strip: ${JSON.stringify(anchors)}`);\n}, 300_000);\n"
}
```

public static bool[] CreateBoolSet(bool defaultState = false, params int[] types)
{
    bool[] array = new bool[TileID.Count];
    for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++) array[i] = defaultState;
    for (int j = 0; j < types.Length; j++) array[types[j]] = !defaultState;
    return array;
}

	public class TileScanner : GenAction
	{
		private ushort[] _tileIds;

		private Dictionary<ushort, int> _tileCounts;

		public TileScanner(params ushort[] tiles)
		{
			_tileIds = tiles;
			_tileCounts = new Dictionary<ushort, int>();
			for (int i = 0; i < tiles.Length; i++)
			{
				_tileCounts[_tileIds[i]] = 0;
			}
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			Tile tile = GenBase._tiles[x, y];
			if (tile.active() && _tileCounts.ContainsKey(tile.type))
			{
				_tileCounts[tile.type]++;
			}
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}

		public TileScanner Output(Dictionary<ushort, int> resultsOutput)
		{
			_tileCounts = resultsOutput;
			for (int i = 0; i < _tileIds.Length; i++)
			{
				if (!_tileCounts.ContainsKey(_tileIds[i]))
				{
					_tileCounts[_tileIds[i]] = 0;
				}
			}
			return this;
		}

		public Dictionary<ushort, int> GetResults()
		{
			return _tileCounts;
		}

		public int GetCount(ushort tileId)
		{
			if (!_tileCounts.ContainsKey(tileId))
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return _tileCounts[tileId];
		}
	}

	public class Blank : GenAction
	{
		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			return UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	public class Custom : GenAction
	{
		private CustomPerUnitAction _perUnit;

		public Custom(CustomPerUnitAction perUnit)
		{
			_perUnit = perUnit;
		}

		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)
		{
			return _perUnit(x, y, args) | UnitApply(origin, x, y, args);
		}
	}

	{
		public override bool Apply(Point origin, int x, int y, params object[] args)

	public static bool SolidOrSlopedTile(Tile tile)
	{
		if (tile != null && tile.active() && Main.tileSolid[tile.type] && !Main.tileSolidTop[tile.type])
		{
			return !tile.inActive();
		}
		return false;
	}

	public static int TileType(int x, int y)
	{
		if (!Main.tile[x, y].active())
		{
			return -1;
		}
		return Main.tile[x, y].type;
	}

	public static bool SolidOrSlopedTile(int x, int y)
	{
		return SolidOrSlopedTile(Main.tile[x, y]);
	}

using Microsoft.Xna.Framework;

namespace Terraria.WorldBuilding;

public static class Searches
{
	public class Left : GenSearch
	{
		private int _maxDistance;

		public Left(int maxDistance)
		{
			_maxDistance = maxDistance;
		}

		public override Point Find(Point origin)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _maxDistance; i++)
			{
				if (Check(origin.X - i, origin.Y))
				{
					return new Point(origin.X - i, origin.Y);
				}
			}
			return GenSearch.NOT_FOUND;
		}
	}

	public class Right : GenSearch
	{
		private int _maxDistance;

		public Right(int maxDistance)
		{
			_maxDistance = maxDistance;
		}

		public override Point Find(Point origin)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _maxDistance; i++)
			{
				if (Check(origin.X + i, origin.Y))
				{
					return new Point(origin.X + i, origin.Y);
				}
			}
			return GenSearch.NOT_FOUND;
		}
	}

	public class Down : GenSearch
	{
		private int _maxDistance;

		public Down(int maxDistance)
		{
			_maxDistance = maxDistance;
		}

		public override Point Find(Point origin)
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < _maxDistance && origin.Y + i < Main.maxTilesY; i++)
			{
				if (Check(origin.X, origin.Y + i))
				{
					return new Point(origin.X, origin.Y + i);
				}
			}
			return GenSearch.NOT_FOUND;
		}
	}

	public class Up : GenSearch
	{
		private int _maxDistance;

		public Up(int maxDistance)
		{
			_maxDistance = maxDistance;
		}

 "content": "// CCCC 批·诊断⑬:全部 style-1 金箱的 countWires(±20)(strip 后基座)——找另 5 组幻影\n// wire 嫌疑 + golden 证据核验。一次性探针。\nimport { it } from 'vitest';\nimport { readFileSync, readdirSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { TILE_DEFS } from '../src/data/tiles';\nimport { TileStore } from '../src/world/TileStore';\n\nconst G2 = '/tmp/sw-slp/g';\nconst BDIR = '/tmp/www-b';\nconst W = 4200, H = 1200, N = W * H;\n\nconst SHEET = new Int32Array(TILE_DEFS.length).fill(-1);\nfor (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n  const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n  if (typeof s === 'number') SHEET[i] = s;\n}\nconst DUMPNAME = new Map<number, string>();\nfor (const f of readdirSync(G2)) { const m = /^(\\d{3})_(.+)\\.typ$/.exec(f); if (m) DUMPNAME.set(Number(m[1]), m[2]); }\nconst gPath = (idx: number, ext: string) => `${G2}/${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${DUMPNAME.get(idx)}.${ext}`;\nconst b8 = (idx: number, e: string) => new Uint8Array(readFileSync(gPath(idx, e)).buffer.slice(0));\nconst b16 = (idx: number, e: string) => new Uint16Array(readFileSync(gPath(idx, e)).buffer.slice(0));\n\nconst itIs = process.env.SW_CCCC === '12';\n\n(itIs ? it : it.skip)('cccc wire suspects', async () => {\n  const gt = b16(100, 'typ'), ga = b8(100, 'act');\n  const fw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/frames.bin`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const wire = new Uint8Array(N);\n  for (let q = 0; q < fw.length; q += 4) wire[fw[q]!] = fw[q + 3]! & 0x1f;\n  for (let y = 517; y <= 541; y++) for (const x of [1122, 1123, 1124]) wire[y * W + x] = 0;   // 已strip\n  const chestList = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/chests.json`, 'utf8')) as Array<{ x: number; y: number }>;\n  const REV = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let i = 0; i < TILE_DEFS.length; i++) {\n    const s = TILE_DEFS[i]?.vanilla?.sheet;\n    if (typeof s === 'number' && !REV.has(s)) REV.set(s, i);\n  }\n  const iw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${BDIR}/s52/ids35.bin`).buffer.slice(0));\n  const ids35 = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let q = 0; q < iw.length; q += 2) ids35.set(iw[q]!, iw[q + 1]!);\n  // 用 frameX 判 style1 金箱:frames.bin 有 fx\n  const fxMap = new Map<number, number>();\n  for (let q = 0; q < fw.length; q += 4) fxMap.set(fw[q]!, fw[q + 1]!);\n  const countWires = (x: number, y: number): number => {\n    let n = 0;\n    for (let i = x - 20; i <= x + 20; i++) {\n      for (let j = y - 20; j <= y + 20; j++) {\n        if (i < 0 || i >= W || j < 0 || j >= H) continue;\n        const b = wire[j * W + i]! & 15;\n        n += (b & 1) + ((b >> 1) & 1) + ((b >> 2) & 1) + ((b >> 3) & 1);\n      }\n    }\n    return n;\n  };\n  let nStyle1 = 0;\n  const suspects: string[] = [];\n  for (let ci = 0; ci < chestList.length; ci++) {\n    const { x, y } = chestList[ci]!;\n    const i = y * W + x;\n    if (!ga[i]! || gt[i]! !== 21) continue;\n    const fx = fxMap.get(i) ?? 0;\n    if (fx % 36 !== 0 || Math.trunc(fx / 36) !== 1) continue;\n    nStyle1++;\n    const n = countWires(x, y);\n    if (n > 0) suspects.push(`#${ci}(${x},${y}) wires=${n}`);\n  }\n  console.log(`style-1 金箱 ${nStyle1} 个;countWires>0 的 ${suspects.length} 个:`);\n  for (const s of suspects) console.log('   ' + s);\n  // 嫌疑箱周边 golden 陷阱证据(57→58/75→76 ±60)\n  const t58 = b16(58, 'typ'), a58 = b8(58, 'act'), t76 = b16(76, 'typ'), a76 = b8(76, 'act');\n  const t57 = b16(57, 'typ'), a57 = b8(57, 'act'), t75 = b16(75, 'typ'), a75 = b8(75, 'act');\n  for (const s of suspects) {\n    const m = /#(\\d+)\\((\\d+),(\\d+)\\) wires=(\\d+)/.exec(s)!;\n    const cx = Number(m[2]), cy = Number(m[3]);\n    const chg = (ta: Uint16Array, aa: Uint8Array, tb: Uint16Array, ab: Uint8Array): string => {\n      const out: string[] = [];\n      for (let y = cy - 60; y <= cy + 60; y++) for (let x = cx - 60; x <= cx + 60; x++) {\n        if (x < 0 || x >= W || y < 0 || y >= H) continue;\n        const i = y * W + x;\n        const va = aa[i]! ? ta[i]! : 0, vb = ab[i]! ? tb[i]! : 0;\n        if (va !== vb) out.push(`(${x},${y})${va}→${vb}`);\n      }\n      return out.slice(0, 10).join(' ');\n    };\n    console.log(`\\n${s}:`);\n    console.log(`   statues57→58: ${chg(t57, a57, t58, a58) || '(无)'}`);\n    console.log(`   traps75→76: ${chg(t75, a75, t76, a76) || '(无)'}`);\n  }\n}, 300_000);\n"
}

glt[i]! + 1 : 0;\n    st.half[i] = ghf[i]!; st.slope[i] = gsl[i]!;\n    st.wire[i] = (gia[i]! ? 0x20 : 0) | wire0[i];\n    st.frameX[i] = fx[i]; st.frameY[i] = fy[i];\n  }\n  restoreGenSolid(gensolid);\n  const gs = structuredClone(gsB) as GenState;\n  __setGenStateForProbe(gs);\n  const rng = new RNG(SEED);\n  for (let q = 0; q < PAD; q++) rng.next();\n  runMicroBiomesPass(st, rng, gs, chests as never);\n  __setGenStateForProbe(null);\n\n  // 八通道对拍\n  const dt = b16(101, 'typ'), da = b8(101, 'act'), dw = b16(101, 'wal'), dl = b8(101, 'liq');\n  const dhf = b8(101, 'half'), dsl = b8(101, 'slope'), dlt = b8(101, 'lty');\n  let dA = 0, dT = 0, dW = 0, dL = 0, dHf = 0, dSl = 0, dLt = 0, dIa = 0;\n  const desc = new Map<number, string>();\n  const pair = new Map<string, number>();\n  let firstI = -1;\n  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {\n    const va = da[i]!, ja = st.flags[i]! & 1;\n    const vt = va ? dt[i]! : 0;\n    const jt = ja ? (SHEET[st.type[i]!] >= 0 ? SHEET[st.type[i]!]! : 9999) : 0;\n    const vl = dl[i]! > 0 ? dlt[i]! + 1 : 0;\n    const jl = st.liquid[i]! > 0 ? st.liquidType[i]! : 0;\n    let d = '';\n    if (va !== ja) { dA++; d += `A${va}>${ja} `; }\n    if (vt !== jt) { dT++; d += `T${vt}>${jt} `; const k = `T${vt}>${jt}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); }\n    if (dw[i]! !== st.wall[i]!) { dW++; d += `W${dw[i]}>${st.wall[i]} `; const k = `W${dw[i]}>${st.wall[i]}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); }\n    if (dl[i]! !== st.liquid[i]!) { dL++; d += `L${dl[i]}>${st.liquid[i]} `; }\n    if (dhf[i]! !== st.half[i]!) { dHf++; d += `Hf `; }\n    if (dsl[i]! !== st.slope[i]!) { dSl++; d += `Sl${dsl[i]}>${st.slope[i]} `; }\n    if (vl !== jl) { dLt++; d += `Lt${vl}>${jl} `; }\n    const via = gia[i]! ? 1 : 0;\n    const jia = (st.wire[i]! >>> 5) & 1;\n    if (via !== jia) { dIa++; d += `Ia `; }\n    if (d) { desc.set(i, d.trim()); if (firstI < 0) firstI = i; }\n  }\n  console.log(`pad=${PAD}: A=${dA} T=${dT} W=${dW} L=${dL} Hf=${dHf} Sl=${dSl} Lt=${dLt} Ia=${dIa} first=(${firstI % W},${Math.floor(firstI / W)})`);\n  console.log(`pairs: ${[...pair.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 12).map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}`);\n  // 聚类\n  const seen = new Uint8Array(N);\n  const clusters: Array<{ cells: number[]; x0: number; x1: number; y0: number; y1: number }> = [];\n  for (const c0 of desc.keys()) {\n    if (seen[c0]) continue;\n    const stack = [c0];\n    seen[c0] = 1;\n    const cells: number[] = [];\n    let x0 = W, x1 = 0, y0 = H, y1 = 0;\n    while (stack.length) {\n      const i = stack.pop()!;\n      cells.push(i);\n      const x = i % W, y = (i / W) | 0;\n      if (x < x0) x0 = x; if (x > x1) x1 = x;\n      if (y < y0) y0 = y; if (y > y1) y1 = y;\n      for (let dy = -1; dy <= 1; dy++) for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {\n        if (!dx && !dy) continue;\n        const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;\n        if (nx < 0 || nx >= W || ny < 0 || ny >= H) continue;\n        const ni = ny * W + nx;\n        if (!seen[ni] && desc.has(ni)) { seen[ni] = 1; stack.push(ni); }\n      }\n    }\n    clusters.push({ cells, x0, x1, y0, y1 });\n  }\n  clusters.sort((a, b) => b.cells.length - a.cells.length);\n  for (let ci = 0; ci < Math.min(clusters.length, 25); ci++) {\n    const cl = clusters[ci]!;\n    const pc = new Map<string, number>();\n    for (const i of cl.cells) for (const tok of desc.get(i)!.split(' ')) pc.set(tok, (pc.get(tok) ?? 0) + 1);\n    console.log(`#${ci} n=${cl.cells.length} bbox=[${cl.x0},${cl.y0}]-[${cl.x1},${cl.y1}] :: ${[...pc.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, 8).map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')} :: eg ${desc.get(cl.cells[0]!)}`);\n  }\n}, 560_000);\n"
}

glt[i]! + 1 : 0;\n    st0.half[i] = ghf[i]!; st0.slope[i] = gsl[i]!;\n    st0.wire[i] = (gia[i]! ? 0x20 : 0) | wire0[i];\n    st0.frameX[i] = fx[i]; st0.frameY[i] = fy[i];\n  }\n  restoreGenSolid(gensolid);\n  for (let pad = 0; pad <= 40; pad++) {\n    const st = new TileStore(W, H);\n    st.type.set(st0.type); st.flags.set(st0.flags); st.wall.set(st0.wall);\n    st.liquid.set(st0.liquid); st.liquidType.set(st0.liquidType);\n    st.half.set(st0.half); st.slope.set(st0.slope); st.wire.set(st0.wire);\n    st.frameX.set(st0.frameX); st.frameY.set(st0.frameY);\n    const gs = structuredClone(gsB) as GenState;\n    __setGenStateForProbe(gs);\n    const rng = new RNG(SEED);\n    for (let q = 0; q < pad; q++) rng.next();\n    const events: Array<[string, unknown]> = [];\n    const G = globalThis as { __swSeg?: (name: string, detail?: unknown) => void };\n    G.__swSeg = (name, detail) => events.push([name, detail]);\n    runMicroBiomesPass(st, rng, gs, chests as never);\n    delete G.__swSeg;\n    __setGenStateForProbe(null);\n    const iceTries = events.filter(e => e[0] === 'iceTry').map(e => e[1] as number[]);\n    const deadOk = events.filter(e => e[0] === 'deadPlace').map(e => (e[1] as number[]).join(',')).join(' ');\n    const nearA = iceTries.find(([x, y]) => Math.abs(x - 1610) <= 8 && y >= 575 && y <= 605);\n    const nearB = iceTries.find(([x, y]) => Math.abs(x - 1127) <= 8 && y >= 683 && y <= 712);\n    console.log(`pad=${pad} iceTries=${iceTries.length} nearA=${nearA ? nearA.join(',') : '-'} nearB=${nearB ? nearB.join(',') : '-'} dead=[${deadOk}]`);\n  }\n}, 560_000);\n"
}

127	    && st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0 && !(st.wire[i]! & WIRE_ACTUATOR);
128	}
129	
130	interface Rect { x0: number; y0: number; x1: number; y1: number }
131	
132	/** 诊断段钩子(CCCC 批):SW_CCCC 探针消费,零开销可选调用 */
133	const segProbe = (name: string, detail?: unknown): void => {
134	  const g = globalThis as { __swSeg?: (name: string, detail?: unknown) => void };
135	  try { g.__swSeg?.(name, detail); } catch { /* probe */ }
136	};
137	
138	export function runMicroBiomesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests?: ChestSink): void {
139	  const g0 = globalThis as { __swMbCap?: (gs: GenState, st: TileStore, chests?: ChestSink) => void };
140	  try { g0.__swMbCap?.(gs, st, chests); } catch { /* probe */ }
141	  segProbe('passStart');
142	  // Skyblock.denyAllGeneration 包裹整个 pass(cs:21787)——1.4.5.6 skyblock 种子全段跳过
143	  if (gs.seedFlags.skyblock) return;
144	  const { w, h } = st;
145	  // ★结构互斥走全局 StructureMap（GenState.protectedRects）：原本 pass 局部

```

'';\n  return key.includes('chest') || key.includes('dresser');\n};\n/** 原版 SolidTile(i,j)(WorldGen.cs:70155-70172):active && 生成期 tileSolid && !tileSolidTop\n *  && !halfBrick && slope==0 && !inActive。countTiles(nextCount cs:9559)消费。\n *  ★曾误用静态 TILE_DEFS.solid 且漏 !inActive——致动格/生成期翻转格(484 等)被误判\n *  实心挡洪水,countTiles<40 拒掉原版收的死箱候选(#101 首差根因,同 WWW 修复①类) */\nfunction solidTileStrict(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  const t = st.type[i]!;\n  return st.flags[i] !== 0 && vanGenSolidType(t) && !TILE_DEFS[t]?.platform\n    && st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0 && !(st.wire[i]! & WIRE_ACTUATOR);\n}",
 "new_string": "/** TileID.Sets.IsAContainer(TileID.cs:405)={21,467,88}——精确 sheet 集\n *  (曾用 key 含 'chest'/'dresser' 子串近似,异 key 边界不可证) */\nconst CONTAINER_TILES = idsByVanillaSheet(21, 467, 88);\nconst isContainer = (t: number): boolean => CONTAINER_TILES.has(t);\n/** Conditions.IsSolid(Conditions.cs:85-97):InWorld(x,y,10) && active && 生成期 tileSolid\n *  ——无 solidTop/half/slope/inActive 排除(与 SolidTile/SolidOrSlopedTile 三者互异) */\nconst condIsSolid = (st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean => {\n  if (x < 10 || y < 10 || x >= st.w - 10 || y >= st.h - 10) return false;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  return st.flags[i] !== 0 && vanGenSolidType(st.type[i]!);\n};\n/** WorldGen.SolidOrSlopedTile(cs:70046):active && 生成期 tileSolid && !tileSolidTop\n *  && !inActive(half/slope 不排除)。Modifiers.IsSolid(Modifiers.cs:551)消费 */\nconst solidOrSlopedAt = (st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean => {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  return st.flags[i] !== 0 && vanGenSolidType(st.type[i]!) && !TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]!]?.platform\n    && !(st.wire[i]! & WIRE_ACTUATOR);\n};\n/** 原版 SolidTile(i,j)(WorldGen.cs:70155-70172):active && 生成期 tileSolid && !tileSolidTop\n *  && !halfBrick && slope==0 && !inActive。countTiles(nextCount cs:9559)/MiningExplosives\n *  首门(cs:13)消费。\n *  ★曾误用静态 TILE_DEFS.solid 且漏 !inActive——致动格/生成期翻转格(484 等)被误判\n *  实心挡洪水,countTiles<40 拒掉原版收的死箱候选(#101 首差根因,同 WWW 修复①类) */\nfunction solidTileStrict(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {\n  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;\n  const i = st.idx(x, y);\n  const t = st.type[i]!;\n  return st.flags[i] !== 0 && vanGenSolidType(t) && !TILE_DEFS[t]?.platform\n    && st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0 && !(st.wire[i]! & WIRE_ACTUATOR);\n}"
}

  // WorldGen.SolidTile(cs:13)\n  {\n    const wl = st.inBounds(ox, oy) ? st.wall[st.idx(ox, oy)] : -1;\n    if (wl === 216 || wl === 187) return false; // 地下沙漠墙门禁(cs:17-20)\n  }\n  const barType = [gs.oreTiers.gold, gs.oreTiers.silver, gs.oreTiers.iron, gs.oreTiers.copper][rng.int(0, 3)];\n  const dir = rng.next() * 2 - 1; k < 40 && !found; k++) {\n      if (condIsSolid(st, ox + k * step, oy)) { x = ox + k * step; found = true; }\n    }\n    if (!found) return false;\n  }\n  // Find(origin, Down(80) + IsSolid)(cs:27-30)——起点即 Right 结果(实心),原版恒原格\n  {\n    let found = false;\n    for (let k = 0; k < 80 && !found; k++) {\n      if (condIsSolid(st, x, y + k)) { y = y + k; found = true; }\n    }\n    if (!found) return false;\n  }\n  // ShapeRunner(10, 20, (dir,1)).Output(shapeData) + Blotches() + Scanner + IsSolid +\n  //   Scanner(cs:31-38)。★Output 挂在形状上=raw 格(Blotches 扩张前)——曾把 blotch\n  //   扩张格塞进 shapeData,矿石地板(All(shapeData))覆盖范围整体走样\n  const data = new Set<number>();\n  let total = 0, solid = 0;\n  {\n    let steps = 20.0, strength = 10.0;\n    let px = x + 0.0, py = y + 0.0;\n    let vx = dir + 0.0, vy = 1.0;\n    while (steps > 0 && strength > 0) {\n      strength = 10 * (steps / 20);\n      steps -= 1;\n      const bx0 = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(px - strength * 0.5)), by0 = Math.max(1, Math.trunc(py - strength * 0.5));\n      const bx1 = Math.min(st.w, Math.trunc(px + strength * 0.5)), by1 = Math.min(st.h, Math.trunc(py + strength * 0.5));\n      for (let i = bx0; i < bx1; i++) {\n        for (let j = by0; j < by1; j++) {\n          if (Math.abs(i - px) + Math.abs(j - py) >= strength * 0.5 * (1 + rng.int(-10, 10) * 0.015)) continue;\n          data.add(j * st.w + i); bi <= ox1; bi++) for (let bj = oy0; bj <= oy1; bj++) pts.push([i + bi, j + bj]);\n          } else {\n            pts.push([i, j]);\n          }\n          // Scanner(obj)→Modifiers.IsSolid(SolidOrSlopedTile)→Scanner(obj2)\n          for (const [qx, qy] of pts) {\n            total++;\n            if (solidOrSlopedAt(st, qx, qy)) solid++;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n      const n8 = Math.trunc(strength / 50) + 1;\n      steps -= n8;\n      px += vx; py += vy;\n      for (let k = 0; k < n8; k++) {\n        px += vx; py += vy;\n        vx += rng.range(-0.5, 0.5); vy += rng.range(-0.5, 0.5);\n      }\n      vx += rng.range(-0.5, 0.5); vy += rng.range(-0.5, 0.5);\n      vx = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vx));\n      vy = Math.max(-1, Math.min(1, vy));\n    }\n  }\n  if (solid < Math.trunc(total / 2)) return false; // cs:35-38\n  const area: Rect = { x0: x - 15, y0: y - 10, x1: x + 15, y1: y + 10 };\n  // structures.CanPlace(area, pad 0)（MiningExplosivesBiome.cs:40）——全局保护图互斥\n  //   + GeneralPlacementTiles 默认内容扫描（单参重载）\n  if (!canPlaceStructure(gs, area, 0, st)) return false;\n  // 矿石地板(cs:44):All(shapeData)+SetTile(type, setSelfFrames)\n  //   SetTile=Clear(~(Wiring|Actuator)) 连墙带液带帧带坡全清后写 type\n  for (const key of data) {\n    const qx = key % st.w, qy = Math.trunc(key / st.w);\n    if (!st.inBounds(qx, qy)) continue;\n    const i = st.idx(qx, qy);\n    st.type[i] = barType; st.flags[i] = 1;\n    st.frameX[i] = 0; st.frameY[i] = 0;\n    st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 0;\n    st.wall[i] = 0; st.liquid[i] = 0; st.liquidType[i] = 0;\n  }\n  // Circle(5) + Blotches 挖房(cs:45):中心 = (origin.X - dir*-5, origin.Y - 5)\n  {\n    const cx = x - Math.trunc(-dir * 5), cy = y - 5;\n    const r = 5, rr = (r + 1) * (r + 1);\n    for (let dy = -r; dy <= r; dy++) {\n      const half = Math.min(r, Math.trunc(Math.sqrt(rr - dy * dy)));\n      for (let dx = -half; dx <= half; dx++) {\n        rng.next();\n        const pts: Array<[number, number]> = [];\n        if (rng.next() < 0.3) {\n          const ox0 = rng.int(-1, 0), ox1 = rng.int(0, 1), oy0 = rng.int(-1, 0), oy1 = rng.int(0, 1);\n          for (let bi = ox0; bi <= ox1; bi++) for (let bj = oy0; bj <= oy1; bj++) pts.push([cx + dx + bi, cy + dy + bj]);\n        } else {\n          pts.push([cx + dx, cy + dy]);\n        }\n        for (const [qx, qy] of pts) {\n          if (!st.inBounds(qx, qy)) continue;\n          const i = st.idx(qx, qy);\n          st.flags[i] = 0;                              // ClearTile(frameNeighbors):\n          st.slope[i] = 0; st.half[i] = 0;              // ClearSlope+active(false)\n          st.wire[i] &= ~WIRE_ACTUATOR;                 // +inActive(false)(Tile.cs:153)\n        }\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  // 两侧落点向下 10 格找地面(cs:47-52):Down(10) 含起点 + Conditions.IsSolid\n  const findDown = (fx: number, fy: number): number => {\n    for (let k = 0; k < 10; k++) {\n      if (condIsSolid(st, fx, fy + k)) return fy + k;\n    }\n    return -1;\n  };\n  const barX = x - (dir > 0 ? 3 : -3);\n  const num3 = rng.int(0, 3) === 0 ? 3 : 7; // cs:48\n  const detX = x - (dir > 0 ? -num3 : num3);\n  let barGround = findDown(barX, y - 3);\n  const detGround = findDown(detX, y - 3);\n  if (barGround < 0 || detGround < 0) return false;\n  barGround--; // result.Y--\n  const detTop = detGround - 1; // result2.Y--\n  // 炸药桶地面 slope/half 复位(cs:55-57)\n  {\n    const gi = st.idx(barX, barGround + 1);\n    st.slope[gi] = 0; st.half[gi] = 0;\n  }\n  // 起爆器下方 3 列整平(cs:58-70):ClearTile + 非实心补石 + slope/half 复位\n  for (let i = -1; i <= 1; i++) {\n    const px = detX + i, py = detTop;\n    if (st.inBounds(px, py)) {\n      const ci = st.idx(px, py);\n      st.flags[ci] = 0; st.slope[ci] = 0; st.half[ci] = 0;\n      st.wire[ci] &= ~WIRE_ACTUATOR;\n    }\n    const below = detTop + 1;\n    if (st.inBounds(px, below)) {\n      const bi = st.idx(px, below);\n      if (!solidOrSlopedAt(st, px, below)) {\n        st.type[bi] = STONE; st.flags[bi] = 1;   // ResetToType(1)+active\n      }\n      st.slope[bi] = 0; st.half[bi] = 0;         // cs:67-68(两分支都复位)\n    }\n  }\n  // PlaceTile(result, 141)(cs:71)——前奏 Clear(锚格 Tile|TilePaint|Slope,cs:59501)+\n  //   Place1x1 门(Wiring.IsAMechanism[141]→下方非巨石族 && SolidTile2(地) && 锚格非活性)\n  {\n    if (st.inBounds(barX, barGround)) {\n      const ai = st.idx(barX, barGround);\n      const wasActive = st.flags[ai] !== 0;\n      st.type[ai] = 0; st.flags[ai] = 0; st.frameX[ai] = 0; st.frameY[ai] = 0;\n      st.slope[ai] = 0; st.half[ai] = 0;\n      const gOK = (() => {\n        if (!st.inBounds(barX, barGround + 1)) return false;\n        const gi = st.idx(barX, barGround + 1);\n        if (st.flags[gi] === 0) return false;\n        const gt = st.type[gi]!;\n        return vanGenSolidType(gt) && !TILE_DEFS[gt]?.platform\n          && st.slope[gi] === 0 && st.half[gi] === 0 && !(st.wire[gi]! & WIRE_ACTUATOR)  // SolidTile2\n          && !BOULDER_TILES.has(gt);                                                   // 非巨石族地板\n      })();\n      if (gOK && !wasActive) {\n        st.type[ai] = EXPLOSIVES; st.flags[ai] = 1;\n        st.frameX[ai] = 0; st.frameY[ai] = 0;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  // 起爆器 411:PlaceTile(..., forced: true)(cs:72)——无视支撑/占位检查强制 2×2 落块\n  for (let dx = 0; dx < 2; dx++) {\n    for (let dy = 0; dy < 2; dy++) {\n      if (!st.inBounds(detX + dx, detTop + dy)) continue;\n      st.setTileSilent(detX + dx, detTop + dy, DETONATOR, dx * 18, dy * 18);\n    }\n  }\n  // WorldUtils.WireLine(WorldUtils.cs:111-131):L 形红线\n  {\n    const sx = Math.min(barX, detX), ex = Math.max(barX, detX);\n    const sy = Math.min(barGround, detTop), ey = Math.max(barGround, detTop);\n    for (let i = sx; i <= ex; i++) {\n      if (st.inBounds(i, barGround)) st.wire[st.idx(i, barGround)] |= WIRE_RED;\n    }\n    for (let j = sy; j <= ey; j++) {\n      if (st.inBounds(detX, j)) st.wire[st.idx(detX, j)] |= WIRE_RED;\n    }\n  }\n  // structures.AddProtectedStructure(area, 5)（MiningExplosivesBiome.cs:74）——pad 5\n  addProtectedStructure(gs, area, 5);\n  return true;\n}"
}
